# The Tide Mother — Video Prompts (STATE 9)

*One video prompt per image prompt (352). Use each image beat as the start frame (image-to-video). Duration equals the beat length. 16:9, 24 fps, photoreal.*


### Sea cliffs, storm, night


**Video 001 · 0:00–0:05 · 5s** (from Image Beat 001)

- **Script segment:** "Maren is heavily pregnant and alone. She stumbles down a cliff path into a sea cave while waves break below."
- **Video prompt:** Photorealistic cinematic dark-fantasy film shot, 16:9, 24 fps, 5 seconds. Start from the reference image (Image Beat 001). Camera: slow aerial glide forward, gradually revealing scale. Action: a tiny cloaked figure with a lantern edging along the cliff path as giant waves explode below, with natural, physically believable movement. Environment: rain lashing sideways, waves exploding against the rocks below, the cloak and hair whipping in the gale. Lighting stays consistent: lightning flashes and a single swinging tallow lantern, deep black shadows, cold blue-grey rim light. Mood: desperate, isolated, dangerous. No dialogue in this shot. Keep faces, costumes and Tal's design identical to the reference frame; no morphing, no text, no extra limbs, no sudden cuts.
- **Camera movement:** slow aerial glide forward, gradually revealing scale
- **Motion:** A tiny cloaked figure with a lantern edging along the cliff path as giant waves explode below; rain lashing sideways, waves exploding against the rocks below, the cloak and hair whipping in the gale
- **Audio:** howling wind, roaring surf, heavy rain, thunder; music: tense low strings
